function s_getLoadTime() {
	if (!window.s_loadT) {
		var b=new Date().getTime(),o=window.performance?performance.timing:0,a=o?o.requestStart:window.inHeadTS||0;

/* returns object value or false dep. if data layer element exists */
function adbCheckDataLayerElement(p) {
	var s=this,pth=[],root=window.dataLayer,pr;
	try {
		if (!root) return false;
		if (typeof p==='string') pth=p?p.split('.'):[];
		else {
			if (Object.prototype.toString.call(p)==='[object Array]') pth=p;
			else if(p) return false;
		}
		while(pr=pth.shift()) {
			try{
				if (pr in root) root=root[pr];
				else return false;
			} catch(e){_satellite.notify("Error in Page Load Rule 'Global Functions' > 'adbCheckDataLayerElement' :"+e);return false;}
		}
	} catch(e){_satellite.notify("Error in 'Global Functions'>function adbCheckDataLayerElement:"+e)}
	return root;
}
/* replaces special cars with their non-encoded values */
function decodeString(s) {
  if (_checkIfExist(s)) {
  	s=s.replace(/&amp;/g,'&');
  	s=s.replace(/&euro;/g,'€');
  	s=s.replace(/\;/g,':');
  	s=s.replace(/\,/g,'-');
  	s=s.replace(/\s{2,}/g,' ');
  }
  return s;
}
/* appends value to list */
function appendToList(L,v,d,u) {
	m=0;
	d=d?d:',';
	if (!L) L='';
	else {
		if (u) {
    	var i,n,a=s.split(L,d);
    	for (i=0;i<a.length;i++) {
        	n=a[i];
        	m=m||(u==1?(n==v):(n.toLowerCase()==v.toLowerCase()));
    	}
		}
	}
	if (!m) L=L?L+d+v:v;
	return L;
}
/* checks and returns value for element */
function _checkIfExist(el,def,lg) {
	def=def!=''?def:'';
  return (typeof(el)=='undefined'||el==='')?'':el;
}

var j=0;
function _getPRODUCTS(obj) {
	if (_checkIfExist(obj)!='') {
		obj=obj.item;
		if (_checkIfExist(obj)!='') {
			if (obj.length>0) {
				_PRODUCTS=[];

				_PRODUCTS['subCategory1ID']=[];

				_PRODUCTS['subCategory4ID']=[];
				_PRODUCTS['subCategory4']=[];
				_PRODUCTS['promotionProductID']=[];
				_PRODUCTS['promotionProductName']=[];
				_PRODUCTS['priceWithTax']=[];
				_PRODUCTS['priceWithoutTax']=[];
				_PRODUCTS['originalPriceWithTax']=[];
				_PRODUCTS['quantity']=[];
				_PRODUCTS['promotionQuantity']=[];
				_PRODUCTS['quantityRequested']=[];
				_PRODUCTS['quantityAdded']=[];
				_PRODUCTS['quantityAvailability']=[];
				_PRODUCTS['orderType']=[];
				_PRODUCTS['position']=[];
				_PRODUCTS['discountTypeEffective']=[];
				_PRODUCTS['default']=[];
				_PRODUCTS['default']['priceWithTax']=0;
				_PRODUCTS['default']['quantity']=0;
				_PRODUCTS['default']['length']=0;
				_PRODUCTS['giftcard']=[];
				_PRODUCTS['giftcard']['priceWithTax']=0;
				_PRODUCTS['giftcard']['quantity']=0;
				_PRODUCTS['giftcard']['length']=0;
				_PRODUCTS['uniqueProductCount']=0;
				for (i=0;i<obj.length;i++) {
					_PRODUCTS['productID'][i]=_checkIfExist(obj[i].productID);
					_PRODUCTS['productName'][i]=_checkIfExist(obj[i].productName);
					_PRODUCTS['productName'][i]=decodeString(_PRODUCTS['productName'][i]);
					_PRODUCTS['subCategory1ID'][i]=_checkIfExist(obj[i].subCategory1ID,'n/a');
					_PRODUCTS['subCategory1'][i]=_checkIfExist(obj[i].subCategory1,'n/a');
					_PRODUCTS['subCategory1'][i]=decodeString(_PRODUCTS['subCategory1'][i]);
					_PRODUCTS['subCategory2ID'][i]=_checkIfExist(obj[i].subCategory2ID);
					_PRODUCTS['subCategory2'][i]=_checkIfExist(obj[i].subCategory2,'n/a');
					_PRODUCTS['subCategory2'][i]=decodeString(_PRODUCTS['subCategory2'][i]);
					_PRODUCTS['subCategory3ID'][i]=_checkIfExist(obj[i].subCategory3ID,'n/a');
					_PRODUCTS['subCategory3'][i]=_checkIfExist(obj[i].subCategory3,'n/a');
					_PRODUCTS['subCategory3'][i]=decodeString(_PRODUCTS['subCategory3'][i]);
					_PRODUCTS['subCategory4ID'][i]=_checkIfExist(obj[i].subCategory4ID,'n/a');
					_PRODUCTS['subCategory4'][i]=_checkIfExist(obj[i].subCategory4,'n/a');
					_PRODUCTS['subCategory4'][i]=decodeString(_PRODUCTS['subCategory4'][i]);
					_PRODUCTS['promotionProductID'][i]=_checkIfExist(obj[i].promotionProductID);
					_PRODUCTS['promotionProductName'][i]=_checkIfExist(obj[i].promotionProductName);
					_PRODUCTS['promotionProductName'][i]=decodeString(_PRODUCTS['promotionProductName'][i]);
					_PRODUCTS['priceWithTax'][i]=_checkIfExist(obj[i].priceWithTax);
					_PRODUCTS['priceWithoutTax'][i]=_checkIfExist(obj[i].priceWithoutTax);
					_PRODUCTS['originalPriceWithTax'][i]=_checkIfExist(obj[i].originalPriceWithTax);
					_PRODUCTS['quantity'][i]=_checkIfExist(obj[i].quantity);
					_PRODUCTS['promotionQuantity'][i]=_checkIfExist(obj[i].promotionQuantity);
					if (i==0) _PRODUCTS['uniqueProductCount']=1;
					else {
						if (_PRODUCTS['productID'][(i-1)]!=_PRODUCTS['productID'][i]) _PRODUCTS['uniqueProductCount']++;
					}
					_PRODUCTS['quantityRequested'][i]=_checkIfExist(String(obj[i].quantityRequested));
					_PRODUCTS['quantityAdded'][i]=_checkIfExist(String(obj[i].quantityAdded));
					_PRODUCTS['quantityAvailability'][i]=_checkIfExist(String(obj[i].quantityAvailability));
					_PRODUCTS['orderType'][i]=_checkIfExist(obj[i].orderType);
					_PRODUCTS['position'][i]=_checkIfExist(obj[i].position);
					if (_ProductIDSGiftCards.indexOf(_PRODUCTS['productID'][i])>-1) {
						_PRODUCTS['discountTypeEffective'][i]='Gift Card';
						_PRODUCTS['giftcard']['length']++;
						_PRODUCTS['giftcard']['quantity']+=Number(_PRODUCTS['quantity'][i]);
						_PRODUCTS['giftcard']['priceWithTax']+=Number(_PRODUCTS['priceWithTax'][i])*Number(_PRODUCTS['quantity'][i]);
					}
          else {
						_PRODUCTS['discountTypeEffective'][i]='n/a'; /* enter default value if needed */
						_PRODUCTS['default']['length']++;
						_PRODUCTS['default']['quantity']+=Number(_PRODUCTS['quantity'][i]);
						_PRODUCTS['default']['priceWithTax']+=Number(_PRODUCTS['priceWithTax'][i])*Number(_PRODUCTS['quantity'][i]);
					}
					_PRODUCTS['default']['priceWithTax']=Math.round(_PRODUCTS['default']['priceWithTax']*100)/100;
					_PRODUCTS['giftcard']['priceWithTax']=Math.round(_PRODUCTS['giftcard']['priceWithTax']*100)/100;
					/* built 3rd party products */
					_PRODUCTS_3rdParty[j]=(_PRODUCTS['promotionProductID'][i]!='')?_PRODUCTS['promotionProductID'][i]:_PRODUCTS['productID'][i];
					j++;
				}
			}
		}
	}
	_DTM.productsLayerSet=1;
	_satellite.notify('A_FUNCTIONS > Object _PRODUCTS CREATED! '+_DTM.productsLayerSet);
}
/* stores value in localStorage OR cookie */
function _setValueToLocalStorageORCookie(n,v,l) {
	try {
		var _storage=window['localStorage'];
		_storage.setItem(n,v);
		_storage.removeItem(n);
		_DTM.storeIndex=1;
	}
	catch(e) {
		_satellite.notify('Error: AppM function "_setValueToLocalStorageORCookie": '+e);}
    if (_DTM.storeIndex) _storage.setItem(n,v);
    else _satellite.setCookie(n,v);
		if (l) _satellite.notify("Write to "+_DTM.storeType[_DTM.storeIndex]+" : "+n+"='"+v+"'");
}
/* reads value from localStorage OR cookie */
function _getValueFromLocalStorageORCookie(n,l) {
	var v='';
	try {v=window['localStorage'].getItem(n);_DTM.storeIndex=1;}
	catch(e){if (v==null||v=='') _satellite.readCookie(n);}
	v=v?v:'';
	if (l) _satellite.notify("Value from "+_DTM.storeType[_DTM.storeIndex]+" for "+n+"='"+v+"'");
	return v;
}
function _getProductFindingMethod() {
	/* define/get vars */
	var _intRef=_satellite.getVar('_isInternalReferrer')=='0';
	var _chnl=s._prevChannel;
	var _primCat=_satellite.getVar('PrimaryCategory');
	var _subCat1=_satellite.getVar('SubCategory_1');
	var _subCat2=_satellite.getVar('SubCategory_2');
	var _subCat3=_satellite.getVar('SubCategory_3');
	var _subCat4=_satellite.getVar('SubCategory_4');
	var _icid=_satellite.getVar('Internal Campaign');
	var _searchPhrase_woCmp=_satellite.getVar('Search - Int Searchphrase wo_cid_icid');
	var _searchPhrase_wCmp=_satellite.getVar('Search - Int Searchphrase w_cid_icid');
	var _refProdID=window._adbRefProdID;
	s.eVar60='';
	/* determine product finding method */
	if (s._pageType=='Product Detail') {
		if (s._prevRefDom!='') s.eVar60='External>Referring Domains>'+s._prevRefDom;
		if (s.products.indexOf('eVar62=')>=0) s.eVar60='Page Type>Product Detail>'+s.prop62.replace(/>/g,'|');
		if (_cartOrderType!='') s.eVar60='Page Type>Cart>'+((_cartOrderType=='reservation')?'Reservation':'Delivery')+' Cart';
		if (document.referrer=='') s.eVar60='External>Unknown>n/a';
		if (s.campaign.match(/^SEA/i)!=null) s.eVar60='External>Paid Search>'+s.campaign;
		if (s.campaign.match(/^(PSM|PSE)/i)!=null) s.eVar60='External>Price Search Engines>'+s.campaign;
		if (s.campaign.match(/^News/i)!=null) s.eVar60='External>Newsletter>'+s.campaign;
		if (s.campaign.match(/^Disp/i)!=null) s.eVar60='External>Display>'+s.campaign;
		if (s.campaign.match(/^Pros/i)!=null) s.eVar60='External>Prospects>'+s.campaign;
		if (s.campaign.match(/^Vid/i)!=null) s.eVar60='External>Video>'+s.campaign;		
		if (s.campaign.match(/^Sms/i)!=null) s.eVar60='External>SMS>'+s.campaign;		
		if (s.campaign.match(/^App/i)!=null) s.eVar60='External>Mobile App>'+s.campaign;		
		if (_chnl.match(/^Social/i)!=null) s.eVar60='External>Social>'+s._prevRefDom;
		if (_chnl.match(/^Natural Search/i)!=null) s.eVar60='External>Natural Search>'+s._prevRefDom;
	}
	if (s._pageType.match(/Product Listing/i)!=null && s._searchPhrase!='') s.eVar60='Onsite Search>Redirect>'+_searchPhrase_woCmp;
	if (s._pageType=='Searchresults') {
		if (_searchPhrase_woCmp!='') s.eVar60='Onsite Search>Searchresults>'+_searchPhrase_woCmp;
		if (_searchPhrase_wCmp!='') s.eVar60='Page Type>Searchresults>'+_searchPhrase_wCmp;
	}
	if (s._pageType.match(/Product Listing|Product Detail/i)!=null && window._autoSuggestCategory!=''&&window._autoSuggestCategory!='n/a'&&window._autoSuggestCategory.match(/product|category/i)) {
		s.eVar60='Onsite Search>Auto-Suggest-Layer>'+_searchPhrase_woCmp+'|'+window._autoSuggestCategory;
	}
	if (s._pageType.match(/Searchresults|Product Listing|Product Detail/i)!=null) {
		if (_icid.match(/^IntHan|IntPro/i)!=null) s.eVar60='Internal Campaign>Online Prospect>'+_icid;
		if (_icid.match(/^IntObj/i)!=null) s.eVar60='Internal Campaign>Object Designer>'+_icid;
		if (_icid.match(/^IntHom/i)!=null) s.eVar60='Page Type>Home>'+_icid;
	}
	if (s._pageType=='Home') s.eVar60='Page Type>Home>n/a';
	if (s.eVar60=='' && s._pageType=='Product Listing') s.eVar60='Page Type>Product Listing>'+_primCat+'|'+_subCat1+'|'+_subCat2+'|'+_subCat3+'|'+_subCat4;
	if (s._pageType=='Product Detail' && window._carouselData!='') s.eVar60='Page Type>Product Detail>'+_refProdID;
	if (s._pageType.match(/Content|no page type set/)!=null) {
		if (s._searchPhrase!='') s.eVar60='Onsite Search>Redirect>'+s._searchPhrase;
		else s.eVar60='Page Type>Content>'+_primCat+'|'+_subCat1+'|'+_subCat2+'|'+_subCat3+'|'+_subCat4;
	}
}

<a href="https://www.facebook.com/bauhaus" target="_new" title="Zu Facebook">
	Zu Facebook</a></li>

<a href="https://www.youtube.com/user/Bauhausinfo" target="_new" title="Zu YouTube">
	Zu YouTube</a></li>
</ul>

<div class="seoW">
<div class="unit size1of1 paddingClass">
<section class="mod modPageExtension">
<h1 class="webfont-bold" style="font-family: 'tazregular', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 24px;">Mehr als ein Baumarkt: BAUHAUS – Fachcentrum mit Online-Shop</h1>
<p></p><p>Die Blätter fallen, es wird wieder kühler: Der Herbst ist die Jahreszeit der fallenden Blätter und kühlen Tage. <strong><a href="https://www.bauhaus.info/laubsauger-laubblaeser/c/10000921">Laubsauger </a></strong>und <strong><a href="https://www.bauhaus.info/haecksler/c/10000920">Häcksler </a></strong>sorgen dafür, dass unser Garten auch in der Herbstzeit glänzt. <strong><a href="https://www.bauhaus.info/geraetehaeuser/c/10001009">Gerätehäuser </a></strong>sorgen für Ordnung im Garten, so hat alles seinen Platz. Denken Sie auch daran, den richtigen <strong><a href="https://www.bauhaus.info/winterschutz-fuer-pflanzen/c/10001015">Winterschutz für Pflanzen</a></strong> zu finden. </p><p>Damit Sie es sich in Ihren vier Wänden schön kuschelig machen können, finden Sie bei uns alles rund um das Thema <strong><a href="https://www.bauhaus.info/heizung/c/10001701">Heizung</a></strong>, <strong><a href="https://www.bauhaus.info/heizkoerper/c/10001683">Heizkörper </a></strong>und vieles mehr. Um Ihre Luft optimal zu halten, denken Sie auch an das richtige Raumklima - hier können <strong><a href="https://www.bauhaus.info/luftbefeuchter/c/10000468">Luftbefeuchter </a></strong>Abhilfe schaffen.</p><p>Bald wird es wieder richtig kalt: Wie wäre es mit einem <strong><a href="https://www.bauhaus.info/kaminoefen/c/10000460">Kaminofen</a></strong>, um Ihrem Zuhause ein besonderes Flair zu geben? Wer sein Immunsystem stärken und sich getreu der finnischen Tradition wärmen möchte, kann auch über eine <strong><a href="https://www.bauhaus.info/saunen/c/10000419">Sauna </a></strong>nachdenken. Da ist Entspannung vorprogrammiert!</p><p></p>
</section>
</div>
</div>

window.ClickTaleSettings.PTC.AssetManager = {
    isActive: true,
    isNeedForImg: false,
    subscriberId: '233624',
    pid: '71',
    storageUrl: 'https://s3.amazonaws.com/nv-p1-s3-assets-01/',
    prefixSpecialCharacters: false,
    getPrefixUrl: function() {
        return this.storageUrl + this.subscriberId + '/' + this.pid;
    },
    getFullURL: function(type, url) {
        var AMUrl = '';
        if (url) {
            switch (type) {
                case 'css':
                    AMUrl = this.getPrefixUrl() + '/CSS/' + url.replace(/:\/\//g, "/").replace(/%20/g, " ");
                    if (this.prefixSpecialCharacters && this.prefixSpecialCharacters.test(AMUrl)) {
                        AMUrl = AMUrl.replace(/\?/g, "%253F").replace(/\&/g, "%26").replace(/\=/g, "%3D");
                    } else {
                        AMUrl = AMUrl.replace(/\?.*/g, "");
                    }
                    break;
                case 'image':
                    AMUrl = this.getPrefixUrl() + '/IMAGE/' + url.replace(/:\/\//g, "/").replace(/%20/g, " ");
                    break;
            }

        }
        return !!AMUrl ? AMUrl : false;
    },
    makeChanges: function(bodyClone) {
        if (this.pid && this.subscriberId) {
            Array.prototype.forEach.call(bodyClone.querySelectorAll('ctdep-link[href][rel*="stylesheet"]'), function(el, ind) {
                var hrefAttr = el.getAttribute('href');
                if (hrefAttr.indexOf(window.ClickTaleSettings.PTC.AssetManager.storageUrl) == -1 && hrefAttr.indexOf('fonts.googleapis.com') == -1) {
                    if (hrefAttr.indexOf('http') == -1) {
                        var hrefLive = document.querySelector('link[href="' + hrefAttr + '"]');
                        if (hrefLive && hrefLive.href) {
                            hrefAttr = hrefLive.href;
                        }
                    }
                    var finalCssURL = window.ClickTaleSettings.PTC.AssetManager.getFullURL('css', hrefAttr);
                    el.setAttribute('href', finalCssURL);
                }
            });
            if (this.isNeedForImg) {
                Array.prototype.forEach.call(bodyClone.querySelectorAll('ctdep-img[src]'), function(el, ind) {
                    var srcAttr = el.getAttribute('src');
                    if (srcAttr.indexOf(window.ClickTaleSettings.PTC.AssetManager.storageUrl) == -1) {
                        if (srcAttr.indexOf('http') == -1) {
                            var srcLive = document.querySelector('img[src="' + srcAttr + '"]');
                            if (srcLive && srcLive.src) {
                                srcAttr = srcLive.src;
                            }
                        }
                        var finalImageURL = window.ClickTaleSettings.PTC.AssetManager.getFullURL('image', srcAttr);
                        el.setAttribute('src', finalImageURL);
                    }
                });
            }
        }
    }
};

window.ClickTaleSettings.PTC.cloneNodeIE9 = function(innerHTML) {
    if (innerHTML instanceof Element) {
        innerHTML = innerHTML.innerHTML;
    }

    var reg = /<(area|base|br|col|command|embed|hr|input|keygen|link|menuitem|meta|param|source|track|wbr|img)[^>]*>/gi;
    var newNode = document.createElement('temp-div');
    var docFrag = document.createDocumentFragment();
    docFrag.appendChild(newNode);
    if (document.documentMode) {
        innerHTML = innerHTML
            .replace(/(<svg[\s\S]+?<\/svg>)/gi, function(m, g1) {
                if (g1) {
                    return '<!--clicktalesvgreplace ' + g1.replace(/<!--[\s\S]+?-->/g, '') + ' -->';
                }
                return m;
            });
    }
    innerHTML = innerHTML
        .replace(/<script\b([^>]*)>([\s\S]*?)<\/script>/gi, '<script><\/script>')
        .replace(/(<div id="?ClickTaleDiv"?[^>]+>)\s*<script[^>]+><\/script>\s*(<\/div>)/i, '$1$2')
        .replace(reg, function(m, g1) {
            if (g1) {
                var checkReg = new RegExp('<' + g1 + '[^\\w]');
                if (checkReg.test(m)) {
                    return m + '<\/' + g1 + '>';
                }
            }
            return m
        })
        .replace(/(<\/?)([A-Za-z])/g, '$1ctdep-$2')
        .replace(/ctdep-style/gi, 'style');

    newNode.innerHTML = innerHTML;

    return newNode;
};

            Enable: true,
            LiveExclude: true,
            AddressingMode: "id",
            OnReadyHandler: function(changeMonitor) {
                changeMonitor.observe();
                if (typeof window.ClickTaleCMSelector === 'string' && window.ClickTaleCMSelector != '') {
                    changeMonitor.exclude({
                        selector: window.ClickTaleCMSelector,
                        multiple: true
                    });
                }

                var CMRemrule = window.ClickTaleSettings.PTC.RulesObjRemoveEls;
                if (CMRemrule) {
                    for (var i = 0; i < CMRemrule.length; i++) {
                        var rule = CMRemrule[i];
                        var CMlocation = rule['location'];
                        if ((!CMlocation || (CMlocation && document.location[CMlocation['prop']].toLowerCase().search(CMlocation.search) != -1))) {
                            if (rule.changeMon) {
                                changeMonitor.exclude(rule.changeMon);
                            }
                            if (rule.changeMonLive) {
                                changeMonitor.exclude({
                                    selector: rule.changeMonLive,
                                    multiple: true
                                });
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            },
            OnBeforeReadyHandler: function(settings) {
                settings.Enable = window.ClickTaleGetUID ? !!ClickTaleGetUID() : false;
                return settings;
            },
            Filters: {
                MaxBufferSize: 1000000,
                MaxElementCount: 10000
            },
            PII: {
                Text: [],
                Attributes: []
            }
        }

        window.ClickTaleSettings.ChangeMonitor.AutoExclude = {
            Enable: true,
            Repeats: 10,
            Interval: 100
        };

        var RulesObj = window.ClickTaleSettings.PTC.RulesObj;
        if (RulesObj) {
            window.ClickTaleSettings.ChangeMonitor.PII.Text = window.ClickTaleSettings.ChangeMonitor.PII.Text || [];
            window.ClickTaleSettings.ChangeMonitor.PII.Attributes = window.ClickTaleSettings.ChangeMonitor.PII.Attributes || [];
            for (var i = 0; i < RulesObj.length; i++) {
                var CMrule = RulesObj[i]['changeMon'];
                var CMlocation = RulesObj[i]['location'];
                if (!CMrule || (CMlocation && document.location[CMlocation['prop']].toLowerCase().search(CMlocation.search) === -1)) {
                    continue;
                }
                var selector = RulesObj[i]['selector'];
                var attributesArr = CMrule.Attributes;
                if (attributesArr instanceof Array) {
                    window.ClickTaleSettings.ChangeMonitor.PII.Attributes.push({
                        selector: selector,
                        transform: (function(attributesArr) {
                            return function(el) {
                                var attrs = el.attributes;
                                var attrsToReturn = {}
                                for (var i = 0; i < attrs.length; i++) {
                                    var name = attrs[i].nodeName;
                                    attrsToReturn[name] = attrs[i].nodeValue;
                                }
                                for (var u = 0; u < attributesArr.length; u++) {
                                    var attr = attributesArr[u];
                                    var attrib = el.getAttribute(attr);
                                    if (typeof attrib === 'string') {
                                        attrsToReturn[attr] = attrib.replace(window.ClickTaleSettings.PTC.textRegex, '-');
                                    }
                                }

                                return attrsToReturn;
                            }
                        })(attributesArr)
                    });
                }
                if (CMrule.Text) {
                    window.ClickTaleSettings.ChangeMonitor.PII.Text.push({
                        selector: [selector, function(elements) {
                            var els = Array.prototype.reduce.call(elements, function(curr, add) {
                                return curr.concat(Array.prototype.slice.call(add.childNodes));
                            }, []) || [];
                            return Array.prototype.filter.call(els, function(child) {
                                return !!(child && child.nodeType === 3);
                            });
                        }],
                        transform: function(el) {
                            return el.textContent.replace(window.ClickTaleSettings.PTC.textRegex, '-');
                        }
                    });
                }
            }
        }
    }
};

window.ClickTaleSettings.Compression = {
    Method: function() {
        return "deflate";
    }
};

window.ClickTaleSettings.Transport = {
    Legacy: false,
    MaxConcurrentRequests: 1,
    BigBuffer: 120000
};

window.ClickTaleSettings.RewriteRules = {
    OnBeforeRewrite: function(rewriteApi) {
        if (window.ClickTaleSettings.PTC.RulesObj) {
            rewriteApi.add(function(buffer) {
                var bodyClone = ClickTaleSettings.PTC.cloneNodeIE9(buffer);

                if (window.ClickTaleSettings.PTC.AssetManager &&
                    window.ClickTaleSettings.PTC.AssetManager.isActive &&
                    typeof window.ClickTaleSettings.PTC.AssetManager.makeChanges == 'function') {
                    window.ClickTaleSettings.PTC.AssetManager.makeChanges(bodyClone);
                }

                var selectorReg = /((?:^|,|\s|>|\+|\~)+)([A-Za-z])/g;

                var RulesObj = window.ClickTaleSettings.PTC.RulesObj;
                for (var i = 0; i < RulesObj.length; i++) {
                    var rewriteApirule = RulesObj[i]['rewriteApi'];
                    var rewriteApilocation = RulesObj[i]['location'];
                    if (!rewriteApirule || (rewriteApilocation && document.location[rewriteApilocation['prop']].toLowerCase().search(rewriteApilocation.search) === -1)) {
                        continue;
                    }
                    var selector = RulesObj[i]['selector'].replace(selectorReg, '$1ctdep-$2');
                    var elements = bodyClone.querySelectorAll(selector);

                    Array.prototype.forEach.call(elements, function(el, ind) {
                        var attributesArr = rewriteApirule.Attributes;
                        if (attributesArr instanceof Array) {

                            for (var u = 0; u < attributesArr.length; u++) {
                                var attr = attributesArr[u];
                                var attrib = el.getAttribute(attr);
                                if (typeof attrib === 'string') {
                                    el.setAttribute(attr, attrib.replace(window.ClickTaleSettings.PTC.textRegex, '-'));
                                }
                            }

                        }
                        if (rewriteApirule.Text) {
                            var children = el.childNodes;
                            Array.prototype.forEach.call(children, function(child) {
                                if (child && child.nodeType === 3) {
                                    child.textContent = child.textContent.replace(window.ClickTaleSettings.PTC.textRegex, '-');
                                }
                            });
                        }
                    });
                }

                var RulesObjRemoveEls = window.ClickTaleSettings.PTC.RulesObjRemoveEls;
                if (RulesObjRemoveEls) {
                    for (var i = 0; i < RulesObjRemoveEls.length; i++) {
                        if (RulesObjRemoveEls[i].rewriteApi) {
                            var elementsToRemove = bodyClone.querySelectorAll(RulesObjRemoveEls[i].rewriteApi.replace(selectorReg, '$1ctdep-$2'));
                            Array.prototype.forEach.call(elementsToRemove, function(el, ind) {
                                if (el.parentNode) {
                                    el.parentNode.removeChild(el);
                                }
                            });
                        }
                        if (RulesObjRemoveEls[i].rewriteApiReplace) {
                            var elementsToReplace = bodyClone.querySelectorAll(RulesObjRemoveEls[i].rewriteApiReplace.replace(selectorReg, '$1ctdep-$2'));
                            Array.prototype.forEach.call(elementsToReplace, function(el, ind) {
                                if (el.parentNode) {
                                    var comment = document.createComment(el.outerHTML);
                                    el.parentNode.replaceChild(comment, el);
                                }
                            });
                        }
                    }
                }

                var reg = /><\/(?:area|base|br|col|command|embed|hr|input|keygen|link|menuitem|meta|param|source|track|wbr|img)>/gi;
                var clone = bodyClone.innerHTML.replace(/ctdep-/gi, '').replace(reg, ' \/>').replace(/<!--clicktalesvgreplace ([\s\S]+?)-->/g, '$1');
                return clone;
            });
        }
        rewriteApi.add({
            pattern: /(<head[^>]*>)/i,
            replace: '$1<script type="text\/javascript" class="cm-ignore" src="https:\/\/cdnssl.clicktale.net\/www\/tc\/jquery.min.js"><\/script>'
        });
    }
};


window.ClickTaleSettings.PTC.doOnlyWhen = function(toDoHandler, toCheckHandler, interval, times, failHandler) {
    if ((!toDoHandler) || (!toCheckHandler)) return;
    if (typeof interval == "undefined") interval = 100;
    if (typeof times == "undefined") times = 10;
    if (--times < 0) {
        if (typeof failHandler === 'function') {
            failHandler();
        }
        return;
    }
    if (toCheckHandler()) {
        toDoHandler();
        return;
    }
    setTimeout(function() {
        window.ClickTaleSettings.PTC.doOnlyWhen(toDoHandler, toCheckHandler, interval, times, failHandler);
    }, interval);
};

function ClickTaleOnRecording() {

    //Adobe Analytics Integration Start
    var adobeAnalyticsOrgID = "9C7CECFF5660226E7F000101@AdobeOrg";

    function clickTaleAdobeIntegration() {
        var uniqueID = ClickTaleGetUID();
        Visitor.getInstance(adobeAnalyticsOrgID).setCustomerIDs({
            "clicktale_userid": {
                "id": uniqueID
            }
        });
        ClickTaleEvent("Adobe Analytics: Unique ID Sent");
    };

    function clickTaleCheckIfAdobeExists() {
        if (window.Visitor && typeof Visitor.getInstance === "function" && typeof Visitor.getInstance(adobeAnalyticsOrgID).setCustomerIDs === "function" && typeof Visitor.getInstance(adobeAnalyticsOrgID).getMarketingCloudVisitorID === "function") {
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    };

    window.ClickTaleSettings.PTC.doOnlyWhen(clickTaleAdobeIntegration, clickTaleCheckIfAdobeExists, 100, 100);
    //Adobe Analytics Integration End
}

function isReadyToRecord() {
    if (typeof ClickTaleUploadPageNow === 'function' && ClickTaleIsRecording()) {
        if (document.querySelector('#categoryNav')) {
            return !!document.querySelector('#categoryNav > li.level1 > ul > li.level2 > ul > li.level3');
        } else {
            return true;
        }
    }
    return false;
};
